<li><span class="flagicon"><span class="mw-image-border" typeof="mw:File"><a href="/wiki/United_States" title="United States"><img alt="United States" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/a/a4/Flag_of_the_United_States.svg/23px-Flag_of_the_United_States.svg.png" decoding="async" width="23" height="12" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/a/a4/Flag_of_the_United_States.svg/35px-Flag_of_the_United_States.svg.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/a/a4/Flag_of_the_United_States.svg/46px-Flag_of_the_United_States.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="1235" data-file-height="650" /></a></span></span> <a href="/wiki/Presidential_Medal_of_Freedom" title="Presidential Medal of Freedom">Presidential Medal of Freedom</a> (2009)</li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Dan_David_Prize" title="Dan David Prize">Dan David Prize</a> (2009)</li>
<li><span class="flagicon"><span class="mw-image-border" typeof="mw:File"><a href="/wiki/United_States" title="United States"><img alt="United States" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/a/a4/Flag_of_the_United_States.svg/23px-Flag_of_the_United_States.svg.png" decoding="async" width="23" height="12" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/a/a4/Flag_of_the_United_States.svg/35px-Flag_of_the_United_States.svg.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/a/a4/Flag_of_the_United_States.svg/46px-Flag_of_the_United_States.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="1235" data-file-height="650" /></a></span></span> <a href="/wiki/Liberty_Medal" title="Liberty Medal">Liberty Medal</a> (2010)</li>
<li><span class="flagicon"><span class="mw-image-border" typeof="mw:File"><a href="/wiki/Kosovo" title="Kosovo"><img alt="Kosovo" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/1f/Flag_of_Kosovo.svg/21px-Flag_of_Kosovo.svg.png" decoding="async" width="21" height="15" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/1f/Flag_of_Kosovo.svg/32px-Flag_of_Kosovo.svg.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/1f/Flag_of_Kosovo.svg/42px-Flag_of_Kosovo.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="840" data-file-height="600" /></a></span></span> <a href="/wiki/Order_of_Freedom_(Kosovo)" title="Order of Freedom (Kosovo)">Order of Freedom</a> (2010)</li>
<li><span class="flagicon"><span class="mw-image-border" typeof="mw:File"><a href="/wiki/United_Kingdom" title="United Kingdom"><img alt="United Kingdom" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/a/ae/Flag_of_the_United_Kingdom.svg/23px-Flag_of_the_United_Kingdom.svg.png" decoding="async" width="23" height="12" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/a/ae/Flag_of_the_United_Kingdom.svg/35px-Flag_of_the_United_Kingdom.svg.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/a/ae/Flag_of_the_United_Kingdom.svg/46px-Flag_of_the_United_Kingdom.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="1200" data-file-height="600" /></a></span></span> <a href="/wiki/Order_of_the_Garter" title="Order of the Garter">Knight Companion of the Most Noble Order of the Garter</a> (2022)</li></ul>
<p>In May 2007, Blair was invested as a <a href="/wiki/Paramount_chief" title="Paramount chief">paramount chief</a> by the chiefs and people of the village of Mahera in Sierra Leone. The honour was bestowed upon him in recognition of the role played by his government in the Sierra Leone Civil War.<sup id="cite_ref-294" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-294">&#91;293&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>On 22 May 2008, Blair received an honorary law doctorate from <a href="/wiki/Queen%27s_University_Belfast" title="Queen&#39;s University Belfast">Queen's University Belfast</a>, alongside Bertie Ahern, for distinction in public service and roles in the Northern Ireland peace process.<sup id="cite_ref-295" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-295">&#91;294&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>On 13 January 2009, Blair was awarded the <a href="/wiki/Presidential_Medal_of_Freedom" title="Presidential Medal of Freedom">Presidential Medal of Freedom</a> by President George W. Bush.<sup id="cite_ref-296" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-296">&#91;295&#93;</a></sup> Bush stated that Blair was given the award "in recognition of exemplary achievement and to convey the utmost esteem of the American people"<sup id="cite_ref-297" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-297">&#91;296&#93;</a></sup> and cited Blair's support for the War on Terror and his role in achieving peace in Northern Ireland as two reasons for justifying his being presented with the award.<sup id="cite_ref-298" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-298">&#91;297&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>On 16 February 2009, Blair was awarded the <a href="/wiki/Dan_David_Prize" title="Dan David Prize">Dan David Prize</a> by Tel Aviv University for "exceptional leadership and steadfast determination in helping to engineer agreements and forge lasting solutions to areas in conflict". He was awarded the prize in May 2009.<sup id="cite_ref-299" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-299">&#91;298&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-300" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-300">&#91;299&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>On 8 July 2010, Blair was awarded the <a href="/wiki/Order_of_Freedom_(Kosovo)" title="Order of Freedom (Kosovo)">Order of Freedom</a> by President <a href="/wiki/Fatmir_Sejdiu" title="Fatmir Sejdiu">Fatmir Sejdiu</a> of Kosovo.<sup id="cite_ref-301" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-301">&#91;300&#93;</a></sup> As Blair is considered to have been instrumental in ending the <a href="/wiki/Conflict_in_Kosovo" class="mw-redirect" title="Conflict in Kosovo">conflict in Kosovo</a>, some boys born in the country following the war have been given the name Toni or <a href="/wiki/Tonibler" title="Tonibler">Tonibler</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-302" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-302">&#91;301&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>On 13 September 2010, Blair was awarded the <a href="/wiki/Liberty_Medal" title="Liberty Medal">Liberty Medal</a> at the <a href="/wiki/National_Constitution_Center" title="National Constitution Center">National Constitution Center</a> in <a href="/wiki/Philadelphia" title="Philadelphia">Philadelphia</a>, Pennsylvania.<sup id="cite_ref-Liberty_303-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Liberty-303">&#91;302&#93;</a></sup> It was presented by former President Bill Clinton, and is awarded annually to "men and women of courage and conviction who strive to secure the blessings of liberty to people around the globe".<sup id="cite_ref-Liberty_303-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Liberty-303">&#91;302&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-304" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-304">&#91;303&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>On 31 December 2021 it was announced that the Queen had appointed Blair a <a href="/wiki/Knight_Companion_of_the_Order_of_the_Garter" class="mw-redirect" title="Knight Companion of the Order of the Garter">Knight Companion of the Order of the Garter</a> (KG).<sup id="cite_ref-305" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-305">&#91;304&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-306" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-306">&#91;305&#93;</a></sup> Blair had reportedly indicated when he left office that he did not want the traditional knighthood or peerage bestowed on former prime ministers.<sup id="cite_ref-307" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-307">&#91;306&#93;</a></sup> A petition cited his role in the Iraq War as a reason to remove the knighthood and garnered more than one million signatures.<sup id="cite_ref-308" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-308">&#91;307&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>He received his Garter insignia on 10 June 2022 from the Queen during an audience at Windsor Castle.<sup id="cite_ref-309" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-309">&#91;308&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
